    Window Window { get; }

    protected virtual AccessibleElement? Navigate(NavigationDirection direction, int fromId)

    protected virtual AccessibleStates? GetState(int childId)

    protected virtual bool? DoDefaultAction(int childId)
    protected virtual bool? Select(int childId, AccessibleSelection flags)
    protected virtual int? GetChildCount()
    protected virtual string? GetDefaultAction(int childId)
    protected virtual string? GetDescription(int childId)
    protected virtual string? GetHelpText(int childId)
    protected virtual string? GetKeyboardShortcut(int childId)
    protected virtual string? GetName(int childId)
    protected virtual string? GetValue(int childId)

    static void NotifyEvent(AccessibleEvent eventType, Window window, int objectId)
